The room is anchored by a textured cream wall behind the bed, a low walnut nightstand holding a ceramic vase with a single stem of eucalyptus, and a wide woven jute rug on the polished oak floor, with a large gilt-framed mirror leaning against the far wall and a half-open door revealing a glimpse of a marble-clad bathroom beyond. The camera is positioned at a front three-quarter angle from the right side of the bed, elevated slightly above eye level in an elevated oblique composition, using an 85mm lens at f/2.0 with Fuji Provia color rendering. A 29-year-old Chinese woman with a sculpted dancer's build lies on her back across the center of the bed, her head resting on a crumpled feather pillow that shows deep compressional folds under her weight.
